# Control interaction inventory

Source-site identifiers include column numbers. An executed event does not prove every state or output is correct; use named tests and assertions. UNEXERCISED sites remain explicit gaps. Current test-file fingerprints exclude evidence from deleted or modified tests. Only passed tests from complete, uniquely identified fresh runs contribute evidence. DOM click means an input event only; handler events demonstrate invocation, neither alone proves correctness. Evidence is accepted only when its SHA-256 source hash matches the current file; stale runs are excluded.

## Slicer inventory amendment — 2026-10-02

HV-007 supersedes the historical slicer labels/order: numbered sound selectors now directly follow the editing waveform; the duplicate selected-sound drag button is removed; destination keys select the target; **Unassign pad sound** replaces **Unassign selected sound** and supports pre-existing assets. Loaded/source-pad drop uses local Replace/Keep; a staged replacement's Unassign restores the original. Add/Split/Delete remain visible at zero sounds. See evidence/hv007-slicer-verification-summary.json. Earlier whole-project counts and line positions are historical, not a freshly regenerated exhaustive inventory.
